Include one sentence each on SD-041 redaction, SD-042 last-write-wins, SD-043 cost guardrail, the Agent UX surface, and the Day-10 60-second demo milestone.\n\n### Step 2: Author Hard Constraints recap (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Add** `## Hard Constraints (from design)` mapping each load-bearing SD (SD-001…SD-043) to the day it lands. Group by week.\n2. **Add** the fail-closed reminder: default `role: ancillary`; only `generate_image` and `logo_ideas` opt in to `variant` per SD-031.\n\n### Step 3: Author Open Question resolutions (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Add** `## Open Question Resolutions`:\n   - **OQ-1** day allocation → see Week 1/2 sections below.\n   - **OQ-2** summarize parallelism → `concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=int(os.environ.get(\"ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY\", \"4\")))` with exponential backoff per `builtin.understand` call.\n   - **OQ-3** prefix line format → width `min(120, $COLUMNS)`, ANSI color iff `sys.stdout.isatty()` and `$NO_COLOR` unset, label truncate to 32 chars with `…`, never truncate IDs. **Stream:** stdout for all prefix lines. **Order:** `[thread]` → `[variants]` → `[hint]` → `Notice:` → `Warn:` → blank line → command output. **Stability:** the prefix-line format is governed by `schema_version`; format changes require a major version bump and a transition period — added to Definition of Done.\n   - **OQ-4** reaper timing → lazy on first index read per process.\n   - **OQ-5** docs path → `docs/threads.md` (matches SD-038).\n   - **OQ-6** quality_floor formula → `data_quality = 0.5 * parent_capture_score + 0.3 * fraction_with_brief_content_sha256 + 0.2 * fraction_with_at_least_one_input_artifact_resolved`, where `parent_capture_score = (runs_with_parents + valid_roots) / total_runs` and a run is a `valid_root` iff `input_artifacts == []`. Floor 0.6 per SD-024. Refusal report names only unresolved-producer runs (never valid roots).\n\n### Step 4: Author Week 1 — Data Layer days 1–5 (`docs/sprint-thread-layer.md`)\n**Scope:** Large\n1. **Add** `## Week 1: Data Layer (Days 1–5)` with `### Day N` subsections (Goal / Deliverables / SDs landed / Acceptance / Tests):\n\n   - **Day 1 — Index foundation + IDs + dependency glue.** Deliverables: `artagents/threads/{ids,index,schema}.py` with `fcntl.flock` (30s acquire timeout; on timeout error message points at `thread gc --force-unlock`) + tmp+fsync+`os.replace` + `.bak` rotate (SD-003), Crockford ULIDs (SD-004), `schema_version: 1` with N-1 reader (SD-039), repo-relative paths only (SD-040). **Append `xxhash>=3.4` to `requirements.txt`** (no pyproject.toml). Acceptance: 8 parallel writes preserve all updates; corrupted file recovers from `.bak`; `pip install -r requirements.txt` resolves cleanly and `import xxhash` succeeds; lock-acquire timeout error message includes `thread gc --force-unlock`. Tests: `tests/test_threads_index.py`, `tests/test_threads_dependencies.py`.\n\n   - **Day 2 — `run.json` + chokepoint wrapper + CLI/request plumbing + redaction + line-ordering + Frozen Schema Review.** Deliverables:\n     - `artagents/threads/{record,wrapper}.py` writing all SD-008 fields.\n     - **Modify `ExecutorRunRequest` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:43`) and `OrchestratorRunRequest` to add `thread: str | None = None`, `variants: int | None = None`, `from_variant: str | None = None`.**\n     - **Modify `artagents/executors/cli.py` `_cmd_run` (`artagents/executors/cli.py:170`) and `run_parser` (`artagents/executors/cli.py:63-79`) to add `--thread <id|@new|@none>`, `--variants N`, `--from <run-id>:<n>`.** Mirror in `artagents/orchestrators/cli.py`.\n     - Wrap `run_executor()` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:75`) and the orchestrator runner with `threads.begin/finalize` per SD-030, with no-op gates for `dry_run`, `tempfile.gettempdir()`, `@none`, `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1`.\n     - **`upload.youtube` short-circuit at `artagents/executors/runner.py:78` is left untouched** and produces zero thread artifacts and zero errors (verified by test).\n     - SD-008 fields: `host_id` (SD-012), `agent_version`, `inputs_digest` via `xxhash`, `output_artifacts[*].sha256` at finalize (SD-032), `external_service_calls` capturing model/model_version/request_id/seed/cost_usd/latency_ms (SD-010), `input_artifacts.produced_by_run_id` resolved at consumption (SD-011).\n     - **Broadened redaction (SD-008 `cli_args_redacted` + extends SD-041 hooks):** any CLI argv key matching the case-insensitive regex `(KEY|TOKEN|SECRET|PASSWORD|PASSPHRASE|API_?KEY|BEARER)` has its value replaced with the literal string `***REDACTED***`. The `secret`-prefixed env-var convention from SD-041 (Day 5) extends this with the env-var path. `runs/<slug>/private/` is treated as opaque (sha-only `input_artifacts` entries with `kind: \"private\"`).\n     - **Line-ordering rule** (Definition of Done item): every prefix block prints to stdout in fixed order — `[thread]` → `[variants]` → `[hint]` → `Notice:` lines → `Warn:` lines → exactly one blank line → command output.\n     - Acceptance: `executors run --thread @new …` and `orchestrators run --thread @new …` create a thread and emit `run.json` with all SD-008 fields populated; `--thread @none` and `ARTAGENTS_THREADS_OFF=1` produce no `run.json` and no `.artagents/` write; `cli_args_redacted` excludes any KEY/TOKEN/SECRET/PASSWORD/etc. value (replaced with `***REDACTED***`); orchestrator nested executor inherits parent thread via env (full env-propagation test on Day 3); **`upload.youtube` invoked through `executors run` produces zero thread artifacts and zero errors**; **prefix-line ordering test passes**. **Frozen Schema Review (end-of-Day-2 milestone): `schema_version=1` is frozen; any later change requires an SD-039 migration helper plus a `.bak` rotation test added BEFORE the change merges.** Tests: `tests/test_threads_record.py`, `tests/test_threads_cli_plumbing.py`, `tests/test_threads_redaction.py` (broadened patterns), `tests/test_threads_upload_youtube_noop.py` (zero thread artifacts, zero errors), `tests/test_threads_line_ordering.py`.\n\n   - **Day 3 — Auto-attribute + prefix lines + nested env propagation + thread show/new/list + agent docs (rebalanced; SD-006/SD-018/SD-038 priority).** Deliverables:\n     - `artagents/threads/{attribute,prefix}.py`: 5-branch decision per SD-006 (lineage inference scans args for `runs/<R>/` paths) under lock; prefix lines per SD-018 + OQ-3 (line-ordering, color, truncation rules).\n     - **Tier firing rules implemented** (the policy itself is documented in the Agent UX Surface section): **Info** = every run; **Notice** = first-run-of-process, gap >1h since last thread run, cwd change, auto-reopen of archived thread; **Warn** = brief novelty (Levenshtein distance vs prior thread brief above a threshold). Warn must fire <5% of runs in dogfood (Day-10 acceptance check).\n     - `python3 -m artagents thread {new, list, show}` (3 of 5 lifecycle subcommands; **`archive` and `reopen` move to Day 4 morning** to relieve Day 3). `thread show @active` accepts `--no-content` per SD-041.\n     - **`thread list` emits a `[thread-health]` smell line** when ≥1 of: any thread has variant groups with 0 kept, ≥3 open threads idle >5d (detection only per SD-036; no auto-fix).\n     - **`ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID` env propagation in `_run_external_executor` (`artagents/executors/runner.py:258`):** the wrapper sets `ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID=<thread_id>` in the child subprocess env; child wrappers skip `threads.begin` if the env var is set and join the parent thread instead. Tests: `tests/test_threads_nested.py` (parent → child inheritance via subprocess).\n     - **SKILL.md paragraph appended (Day 3, not Day 5) per SD-038**, with exact verbatim text:\n       > \"At the start of any session that will produce runs, run `python3 -m artagents thread show @active` first. Selections are append-only; the most recent write is authoritative on read; prior selections are preserved as history but do not affect current keepers (SD-042).     "OQ-2 resolution: ThreadPoolExecutor max_workers=int(env ARTAGENTS_SUMMARIZE_CONCURRENCY or 4) with exponential backoff per call.",

    "OQ-4 resolution: in-flight reaper runs lazily on first index read per process.",
    "OQ-5 resolution: global doc lives at docs/threads.md.",
    "OQ-6 corrected: data_quality = 0.5*parent_capture_score + 0.3*has_brief_sha + 0.2*has_resolved_input_artifact, where parent_capture_score = (runs_with_parents + valid_roots)/total_runs and valid_root iff input_artifacts==[]; floor 0.6.",
    "Three modality renderers per SD-035: image_grid, audio_waveform, generic_card.",
    "Dogfood fixture is runs/artagents_logo_v3 with verified contents.",
    "Day 5 is the latest possible day for shipping SD-008 metadata; hard gate.",

    "IDLE_ARCHIVE_WINDOW=7d, IDLE_REOPEN_WINDOW=48h.",
    "Existing pytest suite must remain green without modification (SD-034).",
    "schema_version starts at 1 for all SD-039 records.",
    "SD-041 brief privacy is opt-in solely via the `runs/<slug>/private/` path convention; no new user-facing flag is added.",
    "SD-008 cli_args_redacted regex is case-insensitive `(KEY|TOKEN|SECRET|PASSWORD|PASSPHRASE|API_?KEY|BEARER)`; matched argv values become `***REDACTED***`.",
    "SD-043 max_iterations default is 200; configurable via --max-iterations flag and ARTAGENTS_ITERATION_MAX env; enforcement lives inside the iteration.summarize executor.",
    "SD-043 summary cache key is (run_id, summarizer_model_version) under .artagents/iteration_cache/.",
    "SD-044 is non-load-bearing context recorded in Out-of-Scope/closing sections.",
    "xxhash version pin is xxhash>=3.4; requirements.txt is the canonical dependency declaration since this repo has no pyproject.toml.",
    "ARTAGENTS_THREAD_ID is the env var children read in _run_external_executor (runner.py:258); when present, child wrapper skips begin and inherits parent thread.",
    "Day-3 risk-day cut-list priority order: defer [thread-health] smell line, then Warn brief-novelty trigger, then thread list; auto-attribute/prefix/nested-env/SKILL.md/inspect-footer are NEVER cut.",
    "Tier firing rules (committed): Info every run; Notice on first-run-of-process / gap >1h / cwd change / auto-reopen of archived thread; Warn on brief-novelty (rare).",
